# Directive Index

[Back to README](../README.md)

Every `D-` (operator directive), `L-` (linguistic finding) and `C-` (correction to the AI)
id from all extraction shards, exactly once: Ron's nine (M1-M8, G1) **179 rows**, and
Matthew's Swahili shards S1-S5 **86 rows** and S6-S11 **321 rows** (229 from the logs, 92
from the Claude Code transcripts) -- see
[Second operator](#second-operator----matthew-swahili-project-claude-swahili). **586 rows.**

Timestamps are as given in the shards. Where a shard gave only a time, the date is
carried from the shard's span. Quotes are trimmed; `(intent)` marks a User-intent field
rather than Ron's own chat text; `(code)` marks a code comment; `(system)` marks a
harness message. Rows that record a bug, crash, tool gap or raw-LCM workaround end with
a `*Status 2026-10-09: ...*` note giving the current state of the fix (shipped, partial or
open, with the version, tool or issue); the row itself still records what happened at the time.

Counts: M1 23 (11 D, 6 L, 6 C) | M2 23 (7/10/6) | M3 27 (7/15/5) | M4 13 (4/6/3) |
M5 32 (15/11/6) | M6 15 (1/10/4) | M7 15 (7/5/3) | M8 18 (7/6/5) | G1 13 (6/4/3).

**Unplaced ids: 0.** Every id below has a landing site.

## Second operator -- Matthew, Swahili (project "Claude-Swahili")

These five shards come from the FlexToolsMCP runtime logs on one machine
(`~/.flextoolsmcp/logs/`, every operation whose `Project:` is Claude-Swahili,
2026-05-21 .. 2026-09-20). The rest of Matthew's Swahili work, from a second machine, is
shards S6-S11 [below](#second-operator-second-machine----s6-s11); many of their `V-` rows
re-check the "(inferred)" claims here.

The log format records submitted code, `User request:` / `User intent:` lines (from
2026-08-12 on only), warnings and errors -- **not** report output. Results below are
therefore read from the code, its comments and docstrings, and the next operation's
framing. `(request)` marks Matthew's verbatim `User request:` text; `(intent)` the
agent-written intent line; `(code)` a code comment or docstring; `(plan)` the phase
plans in `MyFlextool/plans/swahili-noun-phases/` that the logs execute; `(inferred)` a
conclusion the shard draws but the log does not state. Op numbers are per log file.

Shard key:

## Second operator, second machine -- S6-S11

These six shards come from a second machine's `~/.flextoolsmcp/logs/`: every
session whose project is Claude-Swahili, 2026-09-12 .. 2026-09-30. With S1-S5 they cover
both machines. Differences from S1-S5:

- From 2026-09-23 on (S8 last session, S9-S11) the log keeps **tool output**, truncated at
  roughly 1-2 KB per block. S6, S7 and most of S8 keep only code, intents, errors and
  message counts, like S1-S5.
- `(output)` marks a quoted tool result. `(request*)` marks a `User request:` line that
  reads like Matthew's words but may have been filled in by the agent.
- Two extra id types: **`T-`** (tooling evidence, mapped to MERGE-NOTES T-xx) and **`V-`**
  (verification of an earlier S1-S5 claim, open question or README conflict).
- The tables carry an extra **result from output** column.
- Some sessions were driven by other clients (OpenCode with local models; a
  "hermes_tools" agent; an unidentified client on 09-25). Their rows are failure-mode
  evidence, not Matthew's practice; the row text says so.

| Shard | Log file(s) under `~/.flextoolsmcp/logs/` | Dates |
|---|---|---|
| S6 | `2026-09-12/session_215450_*` (main), `231523/231539/231558_*` (proposal subagents), 15 "hermes_tools" sessions | 09-12 21:14 .. 23:50 |
| S7 | `2026-09-13/*` (27 logs, incl. 6 OpenCode sessions) | 09-13 00:04 .. 09-14 13:15 |
| S8 | `2026-09-14/session_131543_*`, `2026-09-20/session_151934/152558/153046/161814_*`, `2026-09-23/session_092942_*` | 09-14 .. 09-23 |
| S9 | `2026-09-24/*` (5 logs) | 09-24 13:35 .. 23:36 |
| S10 | `2026-09-25/*` (12 Claude-Swahili logs; `session_100020_*` excluded, Sena 3 tooling work) | 09-25 00:14 .. 23:32 |
| S11 | `2026-09-30/*` (14 logs) | 09-30 13:28 .. 20:18 |

Counts: S6 39 (12 D, 6 L, 5 C, 9 T, 7 V) | S7 43 (9/9/9/6/10) | S8 42 (14/6/7/9/6) |
S9 38 (6/8/5/11/8) | S10 41 (10/6/6/9/10) | S11 26 (4/3/7/9/3). **229 rows** from the logs.

**Transcript check (2026-10-09).** Each S6-S11 row was re-checked against the Claude Code
transcripts behind the session (Matthew authorized reading them). The verdict
(CONFIRMED / UPDATED / REFUTED) is appended to the result column as **Transcript check**;
rows with no transcript coverage carry none. 92 ids found only in the transcripts are
appended to each shard, marked "(from transcript)": S6 11, S7 29, S8 12, S9 21, S10 10,
S11 9. Where two transcript passes minted the same new id, the later one was renumbered
(S7: D-13..16, L-16..19, C-13..15, T-10, V-12; S10: D-15/16, C-09/10, L-08/09).

Landing sites are the files where the merge placed the evidence (stage "Second-Operator
Evidence" sections, the reference files, conventions, open questions, MERGE-NOTES
section 4 and README 4.11-4.20).

## Unplaced ids

**None.** All 586 ids are placed (179 M/G + 86 S1-S5 + 321 S6-S11).

Six ids are placed as **"recorded, not applied"** because G1 §9 classifies them as
deck-project material that must not shape the grammar-construction stages:
D-G1-01, D-G1-02, D-G1-03, L-G1-01, L-G1-02, L-G1-03. They are listed above with that
disposition stated explicitly, so the decision is auditable rather than silent.
